    "div" : "<div xmlns=\"http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml\"><p class=\"res-header-id\"><b>Generated Narrative: CapabilityStatement PHCoreServerCapabilityStatement</b></p><a name=\"PHCoreServerCapabilityStatement\"> </a><a name=\"hcPHCoreServerCapabilityStatement\"> </a><h2 id=\"title\"/><ul><li>Implementation Guide Version: 0.2.1 </li><li>FHIR Version: 4.0.1 </li><li>Supported Formats: <strong>SHALL</strong> support <code>json</code>, <strong>MAY</strong> support <code>xml</code></li><li>Published on: 2026-10-17 </li><li>Published by: UP Manila National TeleHealth Center </li></ul><blockquote class=\"impl-note\"><p><strong>Note to Implementers: FHIR Capabilities</strong></p><p>Any FHIR capability may be 'allowed' by the system unless explicitly marked as 'SHALL NOT'. A few items are marked as MAY in the Implementation Guide to highlight their potential relevance to the use case.</p></blockquote><h2 id=\"rest\">FHIR RESTful Capabilities</h2><div class=\"panel panel-default\"><div class=\"panel-heading\"><h3 id=\"mode1\" class=\"panel-title\">Mode: <code>server</code></h3></div><div class=\"panel-body\"><div><p>The PH Core Server SHALL support at least one relevant PH Core resource profile. Resource selection is optional; once selected for REST access, its listed profile, read, search and SHALL filters/combinations apply. Patient-centric exchange SHALL support PH Core Patient for the exchanged patient context; provider/facility directories need not implement Patient solely for Server conformance. Create and update remain optional. A Server declaring create SHALL support conditional create; a Server declaring update SHALL support conditional update and version-aware updates. The catalog's conditionalCreate, conditionalUpdate, versioning and transaction SHALL expectations have this documented applicability and do not require read-only Servers to implement writes. The minimum version policy is versioned; versioned-update also satisfies the requirement. Write-enabled ingestion means create or update is supported for at least one selected PH Core resource; such Servers SHALL support atomic transaction Bundles for their supported resources. Servers SHALL support GET and POST search, paging, _count and the search semantics and combinations specified in <a href=\"client-server-conformance.html\">Client and Server conformance</a>. Servers SHALL return conformant JSON, enforce authorization, and fulfill profile constraints and applicable actor obligations. They SHALL publish an implementation-specific kind=instance CapabilityStatement at GET [base]/metadata, use instantiates for this requirements statement, and declare their actual selected PH profiles, supported interactions, conditional/versioning policies, searches, joins, operations, formats and security arrangements. They SHOULD identify conforming profiles in meta.profile. A dual-role implementation MAY instantiate both PH requirements statements with client and server REST blocks. Profile-only conformance does not require the REST contract. Supporting a profile or search does not mandate a program workflow or new data capture.</p>\n</div><div class=\"lead\"><em>Security</em></div><blockquote><div><p>The Server SHALL use HTTPS for production exchange, apply deployment authorization/consent policies and reject unauthorized requests using appropriate FHIR responses. Invalid requests, unknown resources and insufficient authorization SHALL produce appropriate 400, 404 and 401/403 responses, subject to FHIR security behavior. OperationOutcome content SHALL follow FHIR rules. Deployment or downstream guides SHALL define authentication, authorization, consent, trust and audit arrangements.</p>\n</div></blockquote><div class=\"lead\"><em>Summary of System-wide Interactions</em></div><ul><li><strong>SHALL </strong>support the <code>transaction</code>interaction described as follows:<div><p>Required only for write-enabled ingestion Servers, meaning create or update is supported for a selected PH Core resource. Read-only Servers may omit transaction. Supported resource entries SHALL be processed atomically with conditional-create/update semantics, temporary fullUrl reference resolution and a transaction-response. Unsupported or failing entries SHALL fail the transaction without partial persistence.</p>\n</div></li><li><strong>MAY </strong>support the <code>batch</code>interaction described as follows:<div><p>Batch support is optional and does not provide the atomicity of a transaction.</p>\n</div></li><li><strong>MAY </strong>support the <code>history-system</code>interaction described as follows:<div><p>System history is optional; deployments needing deletion-aware synchronization SHALL define an appropriate history/tombstone or equivalent contract.</p>\n</div></li><li><strong>MAY </strong>support the <code>search-system</code>interaction described as follows:<div><p>Cross-resource system search is optional; the baseline requires resource-type search for selected resources.</p>\n</div></li></ul></div></div><h3 id=\"resourcesCap1\">Capabilities by Resource/Profile</h3><h4 id=\"resourcesSummary1\">Summary</h4><p>The summary table lists the resources that are part of this configuration, and for each resource it lists:</p><ul><li>The relevant profiles (if any)</li><li>The interactions supported by each resource (<b><span class=\"bg-info\">R</span></b>ead, <b><span class=\"bg-info\">S</span></b>earch, <b><span class=\"bg-info\">U</span></b>pdate, and <b><span class=\"bg-info\">C</span></b>reate, are always shown, while <b><span class=\"bg-info\">VR</span></b>ead, <b><span class=\"bg-info\">P</span></b>atch, <b><span class=\"bg-info\">D</span></b>elete, <b><span class=\"bg-info\">H</span></b>istory on <b><span class=\"bg-info\">I</span></b>nstance, or <b><span class=\"bg-info\">H</span></b>istory on <b><span class=\"bg-info\">T</span></b>ype are only present if at least one of the resources has support for them.</li><li><span>The required, recommended, and some optional search parameters (if any). </span></li><li>The linked resources enabled for <code>_include</code></li><li>The other resources enabled for <code>_revinclude</code></li><li>The operations on the resource (if any)</li></ul><div class=\"table-responsive\"><table class=\"table table-condensed table-hover\"><thead><tr><th><b>Resource Type</b></th><th><b>Profile</b></th><th class=\"text-center\"><b title=\"GET a resource (read interaction)\">R</b></th><th class=\"text-center\"><b title=\"GET past versions of resources (vread interaction)\">V-R</b></th><th class=\"text-center\"><b title=\"GET all set of resources of the type (search interaction)\">S</b></th><th class=\"text-center\"><b title=\"PUT a new resource version       "mode" : "server",
      "documentation" : "The PH Core Server SHALL support at least one relevant PH Core resource profile. Resource selection is optional; once selected for REST access, its listed profile, read, search and SHALL filters/combinations apply. Patient-centric exchange SHALL support PH Core Patient for the exchanged patient context; provider/facility directories need not implement Patient solely for Server conformance. Create and update remain optional. A Server declaring create SHALL support conditional create; a Server declaring update SHALL support conditional update and version-aware updates. The catalog's conditionalCreate, conditionalUpdate, versioning and transaction SHALL expectations have this documented applicability and do not require read-only Servers to implement writes. The minimum version policy is versioned; versioned-update also satisfies the requirement. Write-enabled ingestion means create or update is supported for at least one selected PH Core resource; such Servers SHALL support atomic transaction Bundles for their supported resources. Servers SHALL support GET and POST search, paging, _count and the search semantics and combinations specified in [Client and Server conformance](client-server-conformance.html). Servers SHALL return conformant JSON, enforce authorization, and fulfill profile constraints and applicable actor obligations. They SHALL publish an implementation-specific kind=instance CapabilityStatement at GET [base]/metadata, use instantiates for this requirements statement, and declare their actual selected PH profiles, supported interactions, conditional/versioning policies, searches, joins, operations, formats and security arrangements. They SHOULD identify conforming profiles in meta.profile. A dual-role implementation MAY instantiate both PH requirements statements with client and server REST blocks. Profile-only conformance does not require the REST contract. Supporting a profile or search does not mandate a program workflow or new data capture.",
